Station: Crafting Table

|---|---|---|

| Empty | Empty | Empty |

Description: ‌ [ Bedrock Edition only ] Maps crafted from only paper do not show the location marker; to add it, a compass must be added to the map. The map keeps its current zoom level, and remembers all of the terrain it has mapped out.

Source: https://minecraft.wiki/w/Crafting/Miscellaneous

## Recipe: Firework Star

Output: 1 Firework Star
Station: Crafting Table
Shape: Shapeless
Ingredients: Gunpowder + Matching Dye + Extra ingredients (optional)

Grid:
| Slot 1 | Slot 2 | Slot 3 |
|---|---|---|
| Empty | Empty | Empty |
| Gunpowder | White Dye / Light Gray Dye / Gray Dye / Black Dye / Brown Dye / Red Dye / Orange Dye / Yellow Dye / Lime Dye / Green Dye / Cyan Dye / Light Blue Dye / Blue Dye / Purple Dye / Magenta Dye / Pink Dye | Head / Gold Nugget / Feather / Fire Charge |
| Glowstone Dust | Diamond | Empty |

Description: More dyes (up to a total of eight) can be added for a mixed color. One head, gold nugget, feather, or fire charge can be added. Both the diamond and the glowstone dust can be added with any of the other ingredients.

Source: https://minecraft.wiki/w/Crafting/Miscellaneous
